Grammar:

Rule 0     S' -> program
Rule 1     program -> _1_decl_repeat
Rule 2     _1_decl_repeat -> _1_decl_items
Rule 3     _1_decl_repeat -> <empty>
Rule 4     _1_decl_items -> _1_decl_items _1_decl_item
Rule 5     _1_decl_items -> _1_decl_item
Rule 6     _1_decl_item -> decl
Rule 7     decl -> class_decl
Rule 8     decl -> func_decl

Rule 14    var_decl -> IDENT IDENT = NEW IDENT ( ) ;
Rule 15    type_spec -> STRING

Rule 24    params -> param_list

Rule 32    stmt_list -> empty

Rule 35    decl_list -> l_decl
